   222	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   223	// The run state.
   224	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   225	
   226	interface Frame {
   227	  /** The declaration path of the node being evaluated (`$.steps.*.role`). */
   228	  readonly decl: DeclPath;
   229	  /** The value path the findings address (`steps.review.role`). */
   230	  readonly path: string;
   231	  readonly value: unknown;
   232	  /** The enclosing container's value — the operand of a `..` selector. */
   233	  readonly parentValue: unknown;
   234	  /** The key of the nearest enclosing OPEN-map entry. */
   235	  readonly ownerKey?: string | undefined;
   236	  readonly label?: string | undefined;
   237	}
   238	
   239	/** What a node evaluation reports upward: whether the node produced a
   240	 * value its dependents may rely on (the implicit container precondition
   241	 * plus every DECLARED `gating`). */
   242	interface NodeResult {
   243	  readonly ok: boolean;
   244	  readonly value?: unknown;
   245	}
   246	
   247	/** A membership lane whose operand has NOT been evaluated yet. The engine
   248	 * defers it to the end of the walk rather than reading an operand whose
   249	 * reliability is still unknown — a GENERAL property of the `memberOf`
   250	 * construct, not a per-rule placement. */
   251	interface DeferredCheck {
   252	  readonly frame: Frame;
   253	  readonly rule: MembershipRule;
   254	  readonly candidate: unknown;
   255	  readonly slots: Slots;
   256	}
   257	
   258	class Run {
   259	  readonly findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
   260	  readonly effectiveConfigs = new Map<string, unknown>();
   261	  readonly runtimeContextBindings: string[] = [];
   262	  /** Declaration path / tag → whether every instance produced a usable
   263	   * value. A rule naming an unreliable operand is suppressed. */
   264	  readonly declOk = new Map<string, boolean>();
   265	  /** Declaration paths whose evaluation has completed. An operand that is
   266	   * neither reliable nor unreliable but PENDING defers its rule. */
   267	  readonly completed = new Set<DeclPath>();
   268	  readonly deferred: DeferredCheck[] = [];
   269	  readonly memo = new WeakMap<object, unknown>();
   270	  root: unknown;
   271	
   272	  constructor(
   273	    readonly channel: EngineChannel,
   274	    readonly catalog: GateCatalog | undefined,
   275	    readonly valueClasses: Readonly<Record<string, NodeDecl>>,
   276	  ) {}
   277	
   278	  emit(path: string, message: string, code?: string): void {
   279	    this.findings.push(code === undefined ? { path, message } : { path, message, code });
   280	  }
   281	
   282	  mark(key: string, ok: boolean): void {
   283	    if (this.declOk.get(key) === false) return;
   284	    this.declOk.set(key, ok);
   285	  }
   286	
   287	  reliable(key: string): boolean {
   288	    return this.declOk.get(key) !== false;
   289	  }
   290	}
   291	
   292	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   293	// Selectors.
   294	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   295	
   296	type SelectorResult =
   297	  | { readonly status: "ok"; readonly values: readonly string[] }
   298	  | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" };
   299	
   300	const UNRELIABLE = { status: "unreliable" } as const;
   301	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   302	
   303	/** Walk a declaration path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open
   304	 * maps. `..` resolves one level up from the citing frame. A prefix that
   305	 * has not been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable". */
   306	function resolvePath(
   307	  run: Run,
   308	  from: Frame,
   309	  path: DeclPath,
   310	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   311	  const segments = path.split(".");
   312	  let nodes: unknown[];
   313	  let declPrefix: string;
   314	  const head = segments.shift();
   315	  if (head === "$") {
   316	    nodes = [run.root];
   317	    declPrefix = "$";
   318	  } else if (head === "^") {
   319	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   320	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   321	    declPrefix = from.decl.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   322	  } else {
   323	    return UNRELIABLE;
   324	  }
   325	  for (const segment of segments) {
   326	    declPrefix = `${declPrefix}.${segment}`;
   327	    if (!run.reliable(declPrefix)) return UNRELIABLE;
   328	    if (!run.completed.has(declPrefix)) return PENDING;
   329	    const next: unknown[] = [];
   330	    for (const node of nodes) {
   331	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   332	      if (segment === "*") {
   333	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   334	          if (typeof key === "string") next.push(child);
   335	        }
   336	      } else {
   337	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   338	        next.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   339	      }
   340	    }
   341	    nodes = next;
   342	  }
   343	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   344	}
   345	
   346	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   347	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   348	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   349	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   350	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   351	    return run.catalog === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   352	  }
   353	  if ("union" in selector) {
   354	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   355	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   356	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   357	    return {
   358	      status: "ok",
   359	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   360	    };
   361	  }
   362	  const target = "keysOf" in selector ? selector.keysOf : "valuesOf" in selector ? selector.valuesOf : selector.collect;
   363	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, target);
   364	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   365	  const values: string[] = [];
   366	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   367	    if ("keysOf" in selector) {
   368	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   369	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   370	    } else if ("valuesOf" in selector) {
   371	      if (!Array.isArray(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   372	      for (const item of node) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   373	    } else {
   374	      if (typeof node !== "string") return UNRELIABLE;
   375	      values.push(node);
   376	    }
   377	  }
   378	  return { status: "ok", values };
   379	}
   380	
   381	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   382	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   383	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   384	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.catalog?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   385	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   386	}
   387	
   388	function checkMembership(
   389	  run: Run,
   390	  frame: Frame,
   391	  rule: MembershipRule,
   392	  candidate: unknown,
   393	  slots: Slots,
   394	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   395	): void {
   396	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   397	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   398	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   399	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   400	    return;
   401	  }
   402	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   403	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   404	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   405	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   406	  if (violated) {
   407	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   408	  }
   409	}
   410	
   411	function parentPath(path: string): string {
   412	  const bracket = path.lastIndexOf("[");
   413	  const dot = path.lastIndexOf(".");
   414	  if (bracket > dot) return path.slice(0, bracket);
   415	  if (dot < 0) return "$";
   416	  return path.slice(0, dot);
   417	}
   418	
   419	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   420	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   420	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   421	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   422	
   423	const PLAIN_DECIMAL = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/;
   424	
   425	/**
   426	 * The `plainDecimalInteger` ladder over a YAML node: alias-free,
   427	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   428	 * the resolved safe-integer belt. Its six messages are FIXED by the
   429	 * ladder (they are identical at every measured user), so they live with
   430	 * the construct rather than being restated per node.
   431	 */
   432	const SOURCE_LADDER = {
   433	  alias: "{path} must not be an alias (an alias hides the source form, probe P21)",
   434	  nonScalar: "{path} must be a plain decimal integer scalar",
   435	  anchor: "{path} must not carry an anchor (the anchor token sits outside the range slice)",
   436	  tag: "{path} must not carry a tag (probe P22: a tag can flip the resolved type silently)",
   437	  form: "{path} must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form {source}",
   438	  resolved: "{path} must resolve to a safe integer >= 1",
   439	} as const;
   440	
   441	export function sourceLadderFinding(node: unknown, source: string, path: string): string | undefined {
   442	  if (node === undefined || node === null) return undefined;
   443	  if (isAlias(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.alias, { path });
   444	  if (!isScalar(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.nonScalar, { path });
   445	  if (node.anchor !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.anchor, { path });
   446	  if (node.tag !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.tag, { path });
   447	  const range = node.range;
   448	  const slice = range ? source.slice(range[0], range[1]) : "";
   449	  if (!PLAIN_DECIMAL.test(slice)) {
   450	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.form, { path, source: JSON.stringify(slice) });
   451	  }
   452	  const resolved: unknown = node.value;
   453	  if (typeof resolved !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
   454	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   455	  }
   456	  return undefined;
   457	}
   458	
   459	/** The YAML-AST address of a value path, for the source-bearing lanes. */
   460	export function astPath(path: string): (string | number)[] {
   461	  const segments: (string | number)[] = [];
   462	  for (const part of path.split(".")) {
   463	    let rest = part;
   464	    const bracket = rest.indexOf("[");
   465	    if (bracket >= 0) {
   466	      const head = rest.slice(0, bracket);
   467	      if (head !== "") segments.push(head);
   468	      rest = rest.slice(bracket);
   469	      for (const match of rest.matchAll(/\[(\d+)\]/gu)) {
   470	        segments.push(Number(match[1]));
   471	      }
   472	      continue;
   473	    }
   474	    segments.push(rest);
   475	  }
   476	  return segments;
   477	}
   478	
   479	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   480	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   481	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   482	
   483	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   484	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   485	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   486	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   487	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   488	  ancestors.add(value);
   489	  try {
   490	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   491	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   492	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   493	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   494	      }
   495	      return undefined;
   496	    }
   497	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   498	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   499	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   500	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   501	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   502	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   503	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   504	      }
   505	    }
   506	    return undefined;
   507	  } finally {
   508	    ancestors.delete(value);
   509	  }
   510	}
   511	
   512	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   513	// Node evaluation.
   514	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   515	
   516	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   517	
   518	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   519	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   520	 * lane in binding #2. */
   521	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   522	
   523	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   524	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   525	}
   526	
   527	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   528	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   529	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   530	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   531	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   532	  return result;
   533	}
   534	
   535	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   536	  switch (decl.kind) {
   537	    case "map.fixed":
   538	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   539	    case "map.open":
   540	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   541	    case "map.plain":
   542	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   543	    case "list":
   544	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   545	    case "string":
   546	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   547	    case "integer":
   548	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   549	    case "enum":
   550	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   551	    case "union":
   552	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   553	    case "raw":
   554	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   555	    case "valueClass":
   556	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   557	    case "delegate":
   558	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   559	  }
   560	}
   561	
   562	function evalValueClassRef(
   563	  run: Run,
   564	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   565	  frame: Frame,
   566	  local?: LocalOk,
   567	): NodeResult {
   568	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   569	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   570	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   571	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   572	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   573	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   574	  return result;
   575	}
   576	
   577	function evalMapFixed(
   578	  run: Run,
   579	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   580	  frame: Frame,
   581	): NodeResult {
   582	  const value = frame.value;
   583	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   584	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   585	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   586	    return { ok: false };
   587	  }
   588	  const container = value;
   589	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   590	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   591	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   592	  // keyset membership.
   593	  const legal = new Set(
   594	    fieldNames.filter((name) => {
   595	      const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   596	      return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   597	    }),
   598	  );
   599	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   600	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   601	
   602	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   603	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   604	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   605	        run.emit(
   606	          frame.path,
   607	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") }),
   608	        );
   609	        continue;
   610	      }
   611	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   612	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   613	      const removed = decl.removedKeys?.[key];
   614	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") };
   615	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   616	    }
   617	  };
   618	
   619	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   620	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   621	    const presence = field?.presence;
   622	    if (field === undefined || presence === undefined) return;
   623	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   624	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   625	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   626	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   627	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   628	      return;
   629	    }
   630	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   631	    run.emit(
   632	      at,
   633	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   634	      presence.code,
   635	    );
   636	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   637	  };
   638	
   639	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   640	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   641	    if (field === undefined) return;
   642	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   643	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   644	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   645	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   646	      return;
   647	    }
   648	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   649	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   650	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   651	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   652	  };
   653	
   654	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   655	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   656	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   657	    emitUnknownKeys();
   658	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   659	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   660	    emitUnknownKeys();
   661	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   662	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   663	  } else {
   664	    emitUnknownKeys();
   665	    for (const name of fieldNames) {
   666	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   667	        emitMissing(name);
   668	        evalOneField(name);
   669	        continue;
   670	      }
   671	      evalOneField(name);
   672	    }
   673	  }
   674	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   675	}
   676	
   677	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   678	  return {
   679	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   680	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   681	    value,
   682	    parentValue,
   683	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   684	  };
   685	}
   686	
   687	function evalMapOpen(
   688	  run: Run,
   689	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   690	  frame: Frame,
   691	): NodeResult {
   692	  const value = frame.value;
   693	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   694	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   695	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   696	    return { ok: false };
   697	  }
   698	  const container = value;
   699	  const entries = entriesOf(container);
   700	  let ok = true;
   701	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && entries.length === 0) {
   702	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   703	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   704	  }
   705	  if (decl.deepKeyStringness !== undefined && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   706	    scanKeyStringness(run, container, frame.path, decl.deepKeyStringness.message, new WeakSet());
   707	  }
   708	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   709	  for (const [key, child] of entries) {
   710	    const keyIsString = typeof key === "string";
   711	    const entryPath = keyIsString ? joinPath(frame.path, key) : frame.path;
   712	    if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) {
   713	      const keyFrame: Frame = {
   714	        decl: `${frame.decl}<key>`,
   715	        path: decl.keyLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : entryPath,
   716	        value: key,
   717	        parentValue: container,
   718	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   719	      };
   720	      const keyResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.keyClass, keyFrame);
   721	      if (!keyResult.ok && decl.keyClass.gating === true) ok = false;
   722	    }
   723	    if (decl.keysSubsetOf !== undefined) {
   724	      const before = run.findings.length;
   725	      // The rule is the OPEN MAP's, so a `^`-relative selector resolves
   726	      // from the map's own container — never from the map itself.
   727	      const subsetFrame: Frame = {
   728	        decl: frame.decl,
   729	        path: entryPath,
   730	        value: key,
   731	        parentValue: frame.parentValue,
   732	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   733	      };
   734	      checkMembership(run, subsetFrame, decl.keysSubsetOf, key, {
   735	        ...slots,
   736	        path: entryPath,
   737	        key,
   738	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   739	      });
   740	      // A key outside the subset is DEAD config: nothing below it has an
   741	      // operand to be validated against (the measured lane's CONTINUE).
   742	      if (run.findings.length > before) continue;
   743	    }
   744	    const entryFrame: Frame = {
   745	      decl: `${frame.decl}.*`,
   746	      path: entryPath,
   747	      value: child,
   748	      parentValue: container,
   749	      ownerKey: keyIsString ? key : frame.ownerKey,
   750	    };
   751	    const entryResult = evaluateNode(run, decl.entry, entryFrame);
   752	    if (keyIsString && entryResult.ok && entryResult.value !== undefined) {
   753	      defineOwn(normalized, key, entryResult.value);
   754	    }
   755	  }
   756	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}.*`);
   757	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   758	}
   759	
   760	/** The gates subtree's file-channel key-STRINGNESS scan: a non-string key
   761	 * cannot become an own property and would blind every own-key scan, so it
   762	 * reports at the NEAREST addressable path (the containing map's). */
   763	function scanKeyStringness(
   764	  run: Run,
   765	  value: unknown,
   766	  path: string,
   767	  message: MessageTemplate,
   768	  seen: WeakSet<object>,
   769	): void {
   770	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return;
   771	  if (seen.has(value)) return;
   772	  seen.add(value);
   773	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   774	    value.forEach((item, index) => scanKeyStringness(run, item, indexPath(path, index), message, seen));
   775	    return;
   776	  }
   777	  if (!isResolvedMap(value)) return;
   778	  for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   779	    if (typeof key !== "string") {
   780	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   780	      run.emit(path, render(message, { path, key, value: key }));
   781	      continue;
   782	    }
   783	    scanKeyStringness(run, child, joinPath(path, key), message, seen);
   784	  }
   785	}
   786	
   787	function evalList(
   788	  run: Run,
   789	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "list" }>,
   790	  frame: Frame,
   791	  local?: LocalOk,
   792	): NodeResult {
   793	  const value = frame.value;
   794	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   795	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
   796	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   797	    return { ok: false };
   798	  }
   799	  let ok = true;
   800	  if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   801	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots));
   802	    if (decl.nonempty.gating === true) ok = false;
   803	  }
   804	  const normalized: unknown[] = [];
   805	  const seen = new Set<unknown>();
   806	  const clean: { readonly member: unknown; readonly path: string }[] = [];
   807	  value.forEach((member, index) => {
   808	    const memberPath = decl.memberLaneAt === "container" ? frame.path : indexPath(frame.path, index);
   809	    const memberFrame: Frame = {
   810	      decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   811	      path: memberPath,
   812	      value: member,
   813	      parentValue: value,
   814	      ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   815	    };
   816	    const before = run.findings.length;
   817	    const result = evaluateNode(run, decl.member, memberFrame, local);
   818	    if (run.findings.length > before) return;
   819	    if (result.value !== undefined) normalized.push(result.value);
   820	    if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   821	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.memberOf, member, { ...slots, path: memberPath, value: member });
   822	    }
   823	    if (decl.unique !== undefined) {
   824	      const at = decl.unique.at === "container" ? frame.path : memberPath;
   825	      if (seen.has(member)) {
   826	        run.emit(at, render(decl.unique.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: member }));
   827	      }
   828	      seen.add(member);
   829	    }
   830	    clean.push({ member, path: memberPath });
   831	  });
   832	  if (decl.disjointFrom !== undefined) {
   833	    for (const entry of clean) {
   834	      const memberFrame: Frame = {
   835	        decl: `${frame.decl}[]`,
   836	        path: entry.path,
   837	        value: entry.member,
   838	        parentValue: value,
   839	        ownerKey: frame.ownerKey,
   840	      };
   841	      checkMembership(run, memberFrame, decl.disjointFrom, entry.member, {
   842	        ...slots,
   843	        path: entry.path,
   844	        value: entry.member,
   845	      });
   846	    }
   847	  }
   848	  run.completed.add(`${frame.decl}[]`);
   849	  return { ok, value: normalized };
   850	}
   851	
   852	function evalString(
   853	  run: Run,
   854	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
   855	  frame: Frame,
   856	  local?: LocalOk,
   857	): NodeResult {
   858	  const value = frame.value;
   859	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
   860	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
   861	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
   862	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
   863	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
   864	    if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) {
   865	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.typeMessage, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   866	      return { ok: false };
   867	    }
   868	  } else {
   869	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   870	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   871	      return { ok: false };
   872	    }
   873	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
   874	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
   875	      return { ok: false };
   876	    }
   877	  }
   878	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   879	    const before = run.findings.length;
   880	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
   881	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
   882	  }
   883	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
   884	}
   885	
   886	function evalInteger(
   887	  run: Run,
   888	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
   889	  frame: Frame,
   890	): NodeResult {
   891	  const value = frame.value;
   892	  if (decl.sourceForm !== undefined && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   893	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(astPath(frame.path), true);
   894	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
   895	    if (message !== undefined) {
   896	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
   897	      return { ok: false };
   898	    }
   899	    return { ok: true, value };
   900	  }
   901	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
   902	  if (belt !== undefined) {
   903	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
   904	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
   905	      return { ok: false };
   906	    }
   907	  }
   908	  return { ok: true, value };
   909	}
   910	
   911	function evalEnum(
   912	  run: Run,
   913	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
   914	  frame: Frame,
   915	): NodeResult {
   916	  const value = frame.value;
   917	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
   918	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
   919	  );
   920	  const slots: Slots = {
   921	    ...baseSlots(frame),
   922	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
   923	  };
   924	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
   925	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
   926	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
   927	  return { ok: false };
   928	}
   929	
   930	function evalUnion(
   931	  run: Run,
   932	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
   933	  frame: Frame,
   934	): NodeResult {
   935	  const value = frame.value;
   936	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   937	  if (typeof value === "string") {
   938	    const removed = decl.removedValues?.[value];
   939	    if (removed !== undefined) {
   940	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
   941	      return { ok: false };
   942	    }
   943	    if (decl.literals?.includes(value) === true) return { ok: true, value };
   944	  }
   945	  if (isContainer(value) && decl.mapCase !== undefined) {
   946	    return evaluateNode(run, decl.mapCase, frame);
   947	  }
   948	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots));
   949	  return { ok: false };
   950	}
   951	
   952	function evalRaw(run: Run, decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "raw" }>, frame: Frame): NodeResult {
   953	  if (decl.containerMessage !== undefined && !isContainer(frame.value)) {
   954	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, baseSlots(frame)));
   955	    return { ok: false };
   956	  }
   957	  return { ok: true, value: materialize(frame.value, run.memo) };
   958	}
   959	
   960	function evalMapPlain(
   961	  run: Run,
   962	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.plain" }>,
   963	  frame: Frame,
   964	): NodeResult {
   965	  const value = frame.value;
   966	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   967	  const realized = materialize(value, run.memo);
   968	  if (!isPlainMap(realized)) {
   969	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, { ...slots, value: realized }));
   970	    return { ok: false };
   971	  }
   972	  if (!isCanonicalizable(realized)) {
   973	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.canonicalJsonSafe.message, slots));
   974	    return { ok: false };
   975	  }
   976	  return { ok: true, value: realized };
   977	}
   978	
   979	function evalDelegate(
   980	  run: Run,
   981	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   982	  frame: Frame,
   983	  local?: LocalOk,
   984	): NodeResult {
   985	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
   986	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
   987	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
   988	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
   989	  const registration = run.catalog?.resolve(by) ?? null;
   990	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
   991	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
   992	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
   993	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
   994	  if (result.ok) {
   995	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
   996	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
   997	  }
   998	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
   999	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1000	    return { ok: false };
  1001	  }
  1002	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1003	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1004	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1005	  }
  1006	  return { ok: false };
  1007	}
  1008	
  1009	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1010	// Cross rules and the deferred queue.
  1011	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1012	
  1013	function unique(values: readonly string[]): string[] {
  1014	  return [...new Set(values)];
  1015	}
  1016	
  1017	/** vocabulary #12's `equals` — two-direction set equality, each direction
  1018	 * carrying its own path grain and wording (ch8-C16's measured form). */
  1019	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1020	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
  1021	  const left = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.left);
  1022	  const right = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.right);
  1023	  if (left.status !== "ok" || right.status !== "ok") return;
  1024	  const declared = unique(left.values);
  1025	  const used = unique(right.values);
  1026	  for (const value of used) {
  1027	    if (declared.includes(value)) continue;
  1028	    const at = render(rule.missingFromLeft.at, { value });
  1029	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromLeft.message, { path: at, value }));
  1030	  }
  1031	  for (const value of declared) {
  1032	    if (used.includes(value)) continue;
  1033	    const at = render(rule.missingFromRight.at, { value });
  1034	    run.emit(at, render(rule.missingFromRight.message, { path: at, value }));
  1035	  }
  1036	}
  1037	
  1038	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1039	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1040	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1041	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1042	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1043	  for (const check of queued) {
  1044	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots);
  1045	  }
  1046	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1047	}
  1048	
  1049	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1050	// Entry points.
  1051	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1052	
  1053	/**
  1054	 * Run a surface declaration over a value graph. ONE computation serves
  1055	 * both channels (D1): the caller supplies `channel`, and the source-bearing
  1056	 * attributes are inert where no source exists.
  1057	 */
  1058	export function runSurface(surface: SurfaceDecl, value: unknown, opts: EngineOptions): EngineRun {
  1059	  const run = new Run(opts.channel, opts.catalog, surface.valueClasses);
  1060	  run.root = value;
  1061	  const rootFrame: Frame = { decl: "$", path: "$", value, parentValue: undefined };
  1062	  const rootDecl = surface.root;
  1063	
  1064	  // The root container precondition: with no map operand nothing below has
  1065	  // an address, so the ONE finding is the whole result. It applies where
  1066	  // the ROOT is declared as a map — a surface whose root is any other
  1067	  // kind evaluates through its own node like every other.
  1068	  const rootIsMap =
  1069	    rootDecl.kind === "map.fixed" || rootDecl.kind === "map.open" || rootDecl.kind === "map.plain";
  1070	  if (rootIsMap && !isContainer(value)) {
  1071	    run.emit("$", render(rootDecl.containerMessage, { path: "$", value }));
  1072	    return {
  1073	      findings: run.findings,
  1074	      normalized: undefined,
  1075	      effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1076	      runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1077	    };
  1078	  }
  1079	
  1080	  // The substrate's `resolve.graph: acyclic` lane. It ACCUMULATES with the
  1081	  // structural lanes (a cycle is not a container, so it suppresses
  1082	  // nothing), and it has an operand only where a resolved graph exists.
  1083	  if (run.channel.kind === "file") {
  1084	    const cycle = findCycle(value, "$", new Set());
  1085	    if (cycle !== undefined) {
  1086	      run.emit(cycle, render(surface.substrate.resolve.graph.message, { path: cycle }));
  1087	    }
  1088	  }
  1089	
  1090	  const result = evaluateNode(run, rootDecl, rootFrame);
  1091	  runDeferred(run);
  1092	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) evaluateEquals(run, rootFrame, rule);
  1093	  return {
  1094	    findings: run.findings,
  1095	    normalized: result.value,
  1096	    effectiveConfigs: run.effectiveConfigs,
  1097	    runtimeContextBindings: run.runtimeContextBindings,
  1098	  };
  1099	}
  1100	
  1101	/** The declaration's own tag inventory — the operand of D4's tag-closure
  1102	 * check (every tag defined is citable, every tag cited is defined). */
  1103	export function collectTags(surface: SurfaceDecl): readonly string[] {
  1104	  const tags: string[] = [];
  1105	  const visit = (decl: NodeDecl): void => {
  1106	    tags.push(decl.tag);
  1107	    switch (decl.kind) {
  1108	      case "map.fixed":
  1109	        for (const field of Object.values(decl.fields)) visit(field);
  1110	        return;
  1111	      case "map.open": {
  1112	        if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) visit(decl.keyClass);
  1113	        visit(decl.entry);
  1114	        return;
  1115	      }
  1116	      case "list":
  1117	        visit(decl.member);
  1118	        return;
  1119	      case "union":
  1120	        if (decl.mapCase !== undefined) visit(decl.mapCase);
  1121	        return;
  1122	      default:
  1123	        return;
  1124	    }
  1125	  };
  1126	  visit(surface.root);
  1127	  for (const [, decl] of Object.entries(surface.valueClasses)) visit(decl);
  1128	  for (const rule of surface.crossRules) tags.push(rule.tag);
  1129	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) tags.push(hook.tag);
  1130	  return tags;
  1131	}

### F4 — A dokumentáltan zárt message-placeholder készletből hiányzik a használt `valueRaw`

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — A deklarációs vocabulary és az engine közötti eltérés, illetve egy félrevezető declaration contract ennek a körnek a magja.

WHAT: A `MessageTemplate` dokumentációja zárt placeholder-készletet állít, de nem sorolja a `valueRaw`-t, miközben az engine külön ágon értelmezi és a kanonikus deklaráció öt helyen használja.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:23`

EVIDENCE:
```text
$ rg -n 'valueRaw' v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts
v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:200:      case "valueRaw":
v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:123:        message: "no gate evaluator is registered for '{valueRaw}'",
v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:134:      beltMessage: "gate evaluator '{valueRaw}' reported a config failure without findings",
v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:423:            message: "round.advanceOnArrivalAt member '{valueRaw}' is not a step",
v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:428:            message: "round.advanceOnArrivalAt member '{valueRaw}' is duplicated",
v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:534:        at: "roles.{valueRaw}",
```

WHY IT MATTERS: Egy deklarációs szerző a „closed placeholder set” alapján a `valueRaw`-ot érvénytelennek tekintené, holott a build helyes message/path parity-je több helyen ettől a rejtett engine-képességtől függ.

   420	// The source-form ladder (vocabulary #6) — file channel only.
   421	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   422	
   423	const PLAIN_DECIMAL = /^[1-9][0-9]*$/;
   424	
   425	/**
   426	 * The `plainDecimalInteger` ladder over a YAML node: alias-free,
   427	 * scalar, anchor-free, tag-free, and a `^[1-9][0-9]*$` raw slice, then
   428	 * the resolved safe-integer belt. Its six messages are FIXED by the
   429	 * ladder (they are identical at every measured user), so they live with
   430	 * the construct rather than being restated per node.
   431	 */
   432	const SOURCE_LADDER = {
   433	  alias: "{path} must not be an alias (an alias hides the source form, probe P21)",
   434	  nonScalar: "{path} must be a plain decimal integer scalar",
   435	  anchor: "{path} must not carry an anchor (the anchor token sits outside the range slice)",
   436	  tag: "{path} must not carry a tag (probe P22: a tag can flip the resolved type silently)",
   437	  form: "{path} must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form {source}",
   438	  resolved: "{path} must resolve to a safe integer >= 1",
   439	} as const;
   440	
   441	export function sourceLadderFinding(node: unknown, source: string, path: string): string | undefined {
   442	  if (node === undefined || node === null) return undefined;
   443	  if (isAlias(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.alias, { path });
   444	  if (!isScalar(node)) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.nonScalar, { path });
   445	  if (node.anchor !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.anchor, { path });
   446	  if (node.tag !== undefined) return render(SOURCE_LADDER.tag, { path });
   447	  const range = node.range;
   448	  const slice = range ? source.slice(range[0], range[1]) : "";
   449	  if (!PLAIN_DECIMAL.test(slice)) {
   450	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.form, { path, source: JSON.stringify(slice) });
   451	  }
   452	  const resolved: unknown = node.value;
   453	  if (typeof resolved !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(resolved) || resolved < 1) {
   454	    return render(SOURCE_LADDER.resolved, { path });
   455	  }
   456	  return undefined;
   457	}
   458	
   459	/** The YAML-AST address of a value path, for the source-bearing lanes. */
   460	export function astPath(path: string): (string | number)[] {
   461	  const segments: (string | number)[] = [];
   462	  for (const part of path.split(".")) {
   463	    let rest = part;
   464	    const bracket = rest.indexOf("[");
   465	    if (bracket >= 0) {
   466	      const head = rest.slice(0, bracket);
   467	      if (head !== "") segments.push(head);
   468	      rest = rest.slice(bracket);
   469	      for (const match of rest.matchAll(/\[(\d+)\]/gu)) {
   470	        segments.push(Number(match[1]));
   471	      }
   472	      continue;
   473	    }
   474	    segments.push(rest);
   475	  }
   476	  return segments;
   477	}

   479	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   480	// The acyclic-graph lane (the substrate's `resolve.graph`).
   481	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   482	
   483	/** DFS with a PATH-SCOPED ancestor set: shared subtrees (legal anchor
   484	 * reuse) are not cycles; only a back-edge is. */
   485	function findCycle(value: unknown, path: string, ancestors: Set<object>): string | undefined {
   486	  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return undefined;
   487	  if (ancestors.has(value)) return path;
   488	  ancestors.add(value);
   489	  try {
   490	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   491	      for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i += 1) {
   492	        const hit = findCycle(value[i], joinPath(path, String(i)), ancestors);
   493	        if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
   494	      }
   495	      return undefined;
   496	    }
   497	    if (isResolvedMap(value)) {
   498	      for (const [key, child] of value.entries()) {
   499	        const segment = typeof key === "string" ? key : "<map-key>";
   500	        const keyHit = findCycle(key, joinPath(path, `${segment}<key>`), ancestors);
   501	        if (keyHit !== undefined) return keyHit;
   502	        const valueHit = findCycle(child, joinPath(path, segment), ancestors);
   503	        if (valueHit !== undefined) return valueHit;
   504	      }
   505	    }
   506	    return undefined;
   507	  } finally {
   508	    ancestors.delete(value);
   509	  }
   510	}

   516	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   517	
   518	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   519	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   520	 * lane in binding #2. */
   521	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   522	
   523	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   524	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   525	}
   526	
   527	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   528	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   529	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   530	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   531	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   532	  return result;
   533	}
   534	
   535	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   536	  switch (decl.kind) {
   537	    case "map.fixed":
   538	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   539	    case "map.open":
   540	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   541	    case "map.plain":
   542	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   543	    case "list":
   544	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   545	    case "string":
   546	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   547	    case "integer":
   548	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   549	    case "enum":
   550	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   551	    case "union":
   552	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   553	    case "raw":
   554	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   555	    case "valueClass":
   556	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   557	    case "delegate":
   558	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   559	  }
   560	}
   561	
   562	function evalValueClassRef(
   563	  run: Run,
   564	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   565	  frame: Frame,
   566	  local?: LocalOk,
   567	): NodeResult {
   568	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   569	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   570	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   571	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   572	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   573	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   574	  return result;
   575	}
   576	
   577	function evalMapFixed(
   578	  run: Run,
   579	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   580	  frame: Frame,
   581	): NodeResult {
   582	  const value = frame.value;
   583	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   584	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   585	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   586	    return { ok: false };
   587	  }
   588	  const container = value;
   589	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   590	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   591	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   592	  // keyset membership.
   593	  const legal = new Set(
   594	    fieldNames.filter((name) => {
   595	      const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   596	      return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   597	    }),
   598	  );
   599	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   600	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   601	
   602	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   603	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   604	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   605	        run.emit(
   606	          frame.path,
   607	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") }),
   608	        );
   609	        continue;
   610	      }
   611	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   612	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   613	      const removed = decl.removedKeys?.[key];
   614	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") };
   615	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   616	    }
   617	  };
   618	
   619	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   620	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   621	    const presence = field?.presence;
   622	    if (field === undefined || presence === undefined) return;
   623	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   624	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   625	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   626	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   627	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   628	      return;
   629	    }
   630	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   631	    run.emit(
   632	      at,
   633	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   634	      presence.code,
   635	    );
   636	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   637	  };
   638	
   639	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   640	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   641	    if (field === undefined) return;
   642	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   643	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   644	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   645	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   646	      return;
   647	    }
   648	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   649	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   650	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   651	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   652	  };
   653	
   654	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   655	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   656	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   657	    emitUnknownKeys();
   658	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   659	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   660	    emitUnknownKeys();
   661	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   662	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   663	  } else {
   664	    emitUnknownKeys();
   665	    for (const name of fieldNames) {
   666	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   667	        emitMissing(name);
   668	        evalOneField(name);
   669	        continue;
   670	      }
   671	      evalOneField(name);
   672	    }
   673	  }
   674	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   675	}
   676	
   677	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   678	  return {
   679	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   680	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   681	    value,
   682	    parentValue,
   683	    ownerKey: parent.ownerKey,
   684	  };
   685	}

   688	  run: Run,
   689	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.open" }>,
   690	  frame: Frame,

     5	 * DATA. This module contains no executable logic: every export is a frozen
     6	 * declaration object consumed by `engine.ts` (the validator) and
     7	 * `normalizer.ts` (the derivation capability, D3).

   480	export const templateFormat: SurfaceDecl = Object.freeze({
   481	  surface: "template-format",
   482	  substrate: {
   483	    read: {
   484	      decode: "strict-utf8",
   485	      message: "invalid UTF-8 byte sequence (templates are strict UTF-8)",
   486	    },
   487	    parse: {
   488	      syntax: "yaml-1.2-core",
   489	      documents: 1,
   490	      duplicateKeys: "reject",
   491	      duplicateKeyMessage: "Map keys must be unique",
   492	      promoteWarnings: true,
   493	      directive: {
   494	        only: "1.2",
   495	        message: "%YAML {key} directive: only YAML 1.2 is supported",
   496	      },
   497	      findingOrder: ["directive", "errors", "warnings"],
   498	    },
   499	    resolve: {
   500	      aliases: "substrate",
   501	      expansionBound: "substrate",
   502	      graph: {
   503	        acyclic: true,
   504	        message: "cyclic value structure: the resolved template graph must be acyclic",
   505	      },
   506	    },
   507	    stages: ["read", "parse", "resolve", "validate", "store"],
   508	    paths: { root: "$", listSegment: "[i]" },
   509	    // ch8-C23: the CLOSED issue-code namespace, disjoint from registry
   510	    // names. `unresolved_context_block_ref` is ratified-but-unbuilt.
   511	    codes: [
   512	      "gate_evaluator_unavailable",
   513	      "runtime_context_required_for_process_gate",
   514	      "invalid_process_gate_config",
   515	      "gate_config_not_supported",
   516	      "unresolved_context_block_ref",
   517	    ],
   518	    internalFailure: { path: "$", message: "internal validator failure: {value}" },
   519	  },
   520	  root: rootNode,
   521	  valueClasses: { idClass, agentConfigValue },
   522	  crossRules: [
   523	    {
   524	      tag: "d-roleset",
   525	      rows: ["ch8-C16"],
   526	      relation: "equals",
   527	      left: { keysOf: "$.roles" },
   528	      right: { collect: "$.steps.*.role" },
   529	      missingFromLeft: {
   530	        at: "roles",
   531	        message: "role {valueJson} is used by steps but not declared",
   532	      },
   533	      missingFromRight: {
   534	        at: "roles.{valueRaw}",
   535	        message: "role {valueJson} is declared but not used by any step",
   536	      },
   537	    },
   538	  ],
   539	  normalizers: [

   541	      tag: "n-advances-round",
   542	      rows: ["ch11-C39", "ch11-C38"],
   543	      hook: "expandAdvancesRound",
   544	      over: "$.steps",
   545	      edges: "transitions",
   546	      advanceSet: "$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt",
   547	      into: "advancesRound",
   548	    },
   549	    {
   550	      tag: "n-effective-config",
   551	      rows: ["ch11-C20", "ch11-C5"],
   552	      hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs",
   553	      over: "$.steps.*.gates",
   554	      carry: ["uses"],
   555	      into: "config",
   556	    },
   557	  ],

     1	import type { GateCatalog } from "../../ports/index.js";
     2	import type { ValidationFinding } from "../errors.js";
     3	import type { EngineChannel } from "./engine.js";
     4	import { astPath, runSurface, sourceLadderFinding } from "./engine.js";
     5	import { normalize } from "./normalizer.js";
     6	import { templateFormat } from "./templateFormat.js";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * The template surface, composed: ONE engine run over the declaration on
    10	 * whichever channel the caller is on, plus the AUDITED RESIDUAL — the
    11	 * lanes that ADR-019 D1 leaves to prose/code — wired into the SAME finding
    12	 * pipeline so the output is one uniform stream.
    13	 *
    14	 * The residual realized here, by audit family:
    15	 *
    16	 * - **R3** the existential cross-rule over resolved registrations
    17	 *   (ch11-C19 → ch12-C5): "IF any binding resolves to a registration whose
    18	 *   `requiresRuntimeContext` is true THEN `$.runtimeContext` must be a
    19	 *   spec map" is a conditional over a DERIVED property of an injected
    20	 *   object; a `when:` general enough to express it is an expression
    21	 *   language.
    22	 * - **R7** the uses-scoped SOURCE ladder on two gate-config integers
    23	 *   (ch11-C12's source half). Its declaration exists — `[vc-authored-int]`
    24	 *   — but realizing it inside the engine requires the `delegate` hand-off
    25	 *   to carry the CHANNEL into the registration, which is a change to the
    26	 *   ratified `GateRegistration` port shape: out of ADR-019 D2's scope and
    27	 *   exactly the "while we are here" D6 forbids. Under D7's ≥2-row test the
    28	 *   alternative — a construct that scopes a source lane by a sibling's
    29	 *   value, serving this one row — is refused, and the row keeps a code
    30	 *   lane. This is R7's first live member; the family was kept open at the
    31	 *   ratification for precisely this case.
    32	 *
    33	 * Not here, and deliberately: **R5** (the store's declared-ref/filename
    34	 * check) stays the store stage's, and **R6** is library-owned wording.
    35	 */
    36	
    37	export interface SurfaceOutcome {
    38	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    39	  /** The admitted VALUE, unbranded: ch11-P2a A6 keeps `admitTemplate` the
    40	   * ONLY sanctioned producer of the `AdmittedTemplate` brand, and that
    41	   * guard is lint-enforced. The engine computes the value; admission
    42	   * brands it. */
    43	  readonly admitted?: unknown;
    44	}
    45	
    46	const RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE = "runtime_context_required_for_process_gate";
    47	
    48	/** R7: the two uses-scoped integer fields whose SOURCE form is laddered by
    49	 * the walk. The scoping is SYNTACTIC — the authored `uses` string, no
    50	 * catalog resolution — exactly as the measured lane does it. */
    51	const SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = {
    52	  "declarative.threshold": ["value"],
    53	  "external.process": ["timeoutMs"],
    54	};
    55	
    56	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown> {
    57	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    58	}
    59	
    60	function entriesOf(
    61	  value: ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>,
    62	): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    63	  if (value instanceof Map) return [...value.entries()];
    64	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]] as const);
    65	}
    66	
    67	function get(value: unknown, key: string): unknown {
    68	  if (!isContainer(value)) return undefined;
    69	  if (value instanceof Map) return value.get(key);
    70	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key] : undefined;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** R7's walk: `steps.<id>.gates.<event>[<i>].config.<field>` for the two
    74	 * scoped fields, on the file channel only (no source, no lane). */
    75	function sourceScopedFindings(value: unknown, channel: EngineChannel): ValidationFinding[] {
    76	  if (channel.kind !== "file") return [];
    77	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
    78	  const steps = get(value, "steps");
    79	  if (!isContainer(steps)) return findings;
    80	  for (const [stepId, step] of entriesOf(steps)) {
    81	    if (typeof stepId !== "string" || !isContainer(step)) continue;
    82	    const gates = get(step, "gates");
    83	    if (!isContainer(gates)) continue;
    84	    for (const [eventType, pipeline] of entriesOf(gates)) {
    85	      if (typeof eventType !== "string" || !Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
    86	      pipeline.forEach((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
    87	        if (!isContainer(binding)) return;
    88	        const uses = get(binding, "uses");
    89	        const config = get(binding, "config");
    90	        if (typeof uses !== "string" || !isContainer(config)) return;
    91	        for (const field of SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS[uses] ?? []) {
    92	          if (get(config, field) === undefined) continue;
    93	          const path = `steps.${stepId}.gates.${eventType}[${String(index)}].config.${field}`;
    94	          const node: unknown = channel.doc.getIn(astPath(path), true);
    95	          const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, channel.source, path);
    96	          if (message !== undefined) findings.push({ path, message });
    97	        }
    98	      });
    99	    }
   100	  }
   101	  return findings;
   102	}
   103	
   104	/**
   105	 * R3: exactly ONE template-grain finding when a resolved process gate
   106	 * demands a runtime context the template does not provision. The lane is
   107	 * a DEPENDENT of the `runtimeContext` node: an illegal value fired its own
   108	 * container finding and left the normalized field absent, which suppresses
   109	 * this one (ch8-C21's container-precondition rule).
   110	 */
   111	function runtimeContextCrossRule(
   112	  normalized: unknown,
   113	  bindings: readonly string[],
   114	): ValidationFinding | undefined {
   115	  if (bindings.length === 0) return undefined;
   116	  const requirement = get(normalized, "runtimeContext");
   117	  if (requirement === undefined || isContainer(requirement)) return undefined;
   118	  return {
   119	    path: "runtimeContext",
   120	    code: RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE,
   121	    message:
   122	      `template declares process gate(s) requiring runtime context but does not ` +
   123	      `declare a provisionable runtimeContext spec { kind, provider } (gates: ${bindings.join(", ")})`,
   124	  };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Run the template surface on ONE channel. All-or-nothing: any finding
   129	 * means no admitted value exists (ch8-C22), and the admitted form is
   130	 * produced by the NORMALIZER, never by the validator (D3).
   131	 */
   132	export function runTemplateSurface(
   133	  value: unknown,
   134	  channel: EngineChannel,
   135	  catalog: GateCatalog,
   136	): SurfaceOutcome {
   137	  const run = runSurface(templateFormat, value, { channel, catalog });
   138	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [...run.findings];
   139	  findings.push(...sourceScopedFindings(value, channel));
   140	  const crossRule = runtimeContextCrossRule(run.normalized, run.runtimeContextBindings);
   141	  if (crossRule !== undefined) findings.push(crossRule);
   142	  if (findings.length > 0) return { findings };
   143	  return { findings: [], admitted: normalize(templateFormat, run.normalized, run.effectiveConfigs) };
   144	}
     1	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     5	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     6	 *
     7	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     8	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
     9	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    10	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    11	 *
    12	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    13	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    14	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    15	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    16	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    17	 *
    18	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    19	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    20	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    21	 */
    22	
    23	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    24	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    25	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    26	}
    27	
    28	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    29	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    33	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Resolve a declared operand path (`$.steps`, `$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt`)
    37	 * over the normalized value. */
    38	function at(root: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    39	  let cursor: unknown = root;
    40	  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    41	    if (segment === "$") continue;
    42	    if (!isRecord(cursor)) return undefined;
    43	    cursor = ownGet(cursor, segment);
    44	  }
    45	  return cursor;
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    50	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    51	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    52	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    53	 * the input carried.
    54	 */
    55	function expandFlagMaps(
    56	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    57	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    58	): void {
    59	  const entries = at(root, hook.over);
    60	  if (!isRecord(entries)) return;
    61	  const declared = at(root, hook.advanceSet);
    62	  const advancing = new Set<string>(Array.isArray(declared) ? (declared as string[]) : []);
    63	  for (const key of Object.keys(entries)) {
    64	    const entry = ownGet(entries, key);
    65	    if (!isRecord(entry)) continue;
    66	    const edges = ownGet(entry, hook.edges);
    67	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    68	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    69	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    70	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    defineOwn(entry, hook.into, flags);
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    79	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    80	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    81	 * config form.
    82	 */
    83	function materializeConfigs(
    84	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    85	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    86	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    87	): void {
    88	  // `over` addresses the pipeline map through two open-map levels:
    89	  // `$.steps.*.gates` — the entry key and the event key are the wildcards.
    90	  const [ownerPath, ...rest] = hook.over.split(".*.");
    91	  const owners = at(root, ownerPath ?? "$");
    92	  if (!isRecord(owners)) return;
    93	  const inner = rest.join(".*.");
    94	  for (const ownerKey of Object.keys(owners)) {
    95	    const owner = ownGet(owners, ownerKey);
    96	    if (!isRecord(owner)) continue;
    97	    const pipelines = ownGet(owner, inner);
    98	    if (!isRecord(pipelines)) continue;
    99	    const rebuiltPipelines: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   100	    for (const eventKey of Object.keys(pipelines)) {
   101	      const pipeline = ownGet(pipelines, eventKey);
   102	      if (!Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
   103	      const base = `${ownerPath === "$" ? "" : `${(ownerPath ?? "").replace("$.", "")}.`}${ownerKey}.${inner}.${eventKey}`;
   104	      defineOwn(
   105	        rebuiltPipelines,
   106	        eventKey,
   107	        pipeline.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   108	          const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	          if (isRecord(binding)) {
   110	            for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   111	          }
   112	          defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(`${base}[${String(index)}]`));
   113	          return carried;
   114	        }),
   115	      );
   116	    }
   117	    defineOwn(owner, inner, rebuiltPipelines);
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}
