     1	import { isAlias, isMap, isPair, isScalar, isSeq, LineCounter, parseDocument } from "yaml";
     2	import type { Document, ParsedNode, YAMLError } from "yaml";
     3	
     4	import type { GateCatalog } from "../ports/index.js";
     5	import { admitFromSource } from "./admit.js";
     6	import type { PipelineFinding, TemplateLoadResult, ValidationFinding } from "./errors.js";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * Packet ch8-P1 (G1/draft C36): the staged load pipeline over raw
    10	 * bytes — read (strict UTF-8 decode) → parse (the document step AND
    11	 * the directive check) → resolve (`toJS`, where the alias-amplification
    12	 * guard fires) → validate. Each stage SHORT-CIRCUITS: the first
    13	 * failing stage's finding(s) are the entire result; every stage's
    14	 * unexpected throw is caught and mapped (no load input produces an
    15	 * uncaught throw — E3/draft C22 binds every stage). The OS half of
    16	 * the read stage belongs to the CALLER that reads files (the store;
    17	 * P2's dev validate) — `opts.path` stamps read-stage findings per
    18	 * E5's presence scoping.
    19	 */
    20	export interface LoadTemplateOptions {
    21	  readonly path?: string;
    22	  /**
    23	   * The gate catalog admission resolves `uses` against (ch11-P2a, A1).
    24	   * OPTIONAL: absent it defaults to the empty catalog — vacuous over a
    25	   * gate-free template (every `uses` resolves unknown). Since ch11-P4 a
    26	   * loadable YAML CAN author `gates`, so a file with gate bindings needs
    27	   * the real catalog to admit; the empty-catalog default stays exact for
    28	   * gate-free files. File callers (the store, the dev validate verb) pass
    29	   * the real catalog.
    30	   */
    31	  readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
    32	}
    33	
    34	/** The empty catalog: every `uses` is unknown. Vacuous over zero
    35	 * bindings (every gate-free template) — A8's byte-identical default. */
    36	const EMPTY_CATALOG: GateCatalog = { resolve: () => null };
    37	
    38	function fail(stage: "read" | "parse" | "resolve", findings: readonly PipelineFinding[]): TemplateLoadResult;
    39	function fail(stage: "validate", findings: readonly ValidationFinding[]): TemplateLoadResult;
    40	function fail(
    41	  stage: "read" | "parse" | "resolve" | "validate",
    42	  findings: readonly PipelineFinding[] | readonly ValidationFinding[],
    43	): TemplateLoadResult {
    44	  return { ok: false, error: { stage, findings } };
    45	}
    46	
    47	function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
    48	  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    49	}
    50	
    51	function nodeAnchor(value: unknown): string | undefined {
    52	  if (!(isScalar(value) || isMap(value) || isSeq(value)) || !("anchor" in value)) return undefined;
    53	  return typeof value.anchor === "string" ? value.anchor : undefined;
    54	}
    55	
    56	type ComparedNodePairs = WeakMap<object, WeakSet<object>>;
    57	
    58	function markCompared(a: unknown, b: unknown, compared: ComparedNodePairs): boolean {
    59	  if (typeof a !== "object" || a === null || typeof b !== "object" || b === null) return false;
    60	  const matches = compared.get(a);
    61	  if (matches?.has(b) === true) return true;
    62	  if (matches) matches.add(b);
    63	  else compared.set(a, new WeakSet([b]));
    64	  return false;
    65	}
    66	
    67	function resolveAlias(value: unknown, doc: Document | undefined): unknown {
    68	  return doc && isAlias(value) ? (value.resolve(doc) ?? value) : value;
    69	}
    70	
    71	function sameValueZero(a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean {
    72	  return a === b || (a !== a && b !== b);
    73	}
    74	
    75	function yamlNodeEqual(
    76	  left: unknown,
    77	  right: unknown,
    78	  doc?: Document,
    79	  compared: ComparedNodePairs = new WeakMap(),
    80	): boolean {
    81	  const a = resolveAlias(left, doc);
    82	  const b = resolveAlias(right, doc);
    83	  if (a === b) return true;
    84	  if (markCompared(a, b, compared)) return true;
    85	  // SameValueZero, not Object.is: the comparator's equality must be at
    86	  // least as coarse as the downstream Map's KEY identity (SameValueZero),
    87	  // or a distinct-judged pair collapses silently after the gate — the
    88	  // integration re-check's 0/-0 catch (Object.is(0, -0) is false, the
    89	  // Map still merges them). YAML canonical int equality also makes
    90	  // 0 == -0; NaN == NaN holds under both forms.
    91	  if (isScalar(a) && isScalar(b)) return sameValueZero(a.value, b.value);
    92	  if (isAlias(a) && isAlias(b)) return a.source === b.source;
    93	  if (isAlias(a) && nodeAnchor(b) !== undefined) return nodeAnchor(b) === a.source;
    94	  if (nodeAnchor(a) !== undefined && isAlias(b)) return nodeAnchor(a) === b.source;
    95	  if (isPair(a) && isPair(b)) {
    96	    return yamlNodeEqual(a.key, b.key, doc, compared) && yamlNodeEqual(a.value, b.value, doc, compared);
    97	  }
    98	  if (isSeq(a) && isSeq(b)) {
    99	    return a.items.length === b.items.length
   100	      && a.items.every((item, index) => yamlNodeEqual(item, b.items[index], doc, compared));
   101	  }
   102	  if (isMap(a) && isMap(b)) {
   103	    if (a.items.length !== b.items.length) return false;
   104	    const matched = new Set<number>();
   105	    return a.items.every((pair) => {
   106	      const index = b.items.findIndex((candidate, candidateIndex) => (
   107	        !matched.has(candidateIndex) && yamlNodeEqual(pair, candidate, doc, compared)
   108	      ));
   109	      if (index < 0) return false;
   110	      matched.add(index);
   111	      return true;
   112	    });
   113	  }
   114	  return false;
   115	}
   116	
   117	interface PositionedFinding {
   118	  readonly offset: number;
   119	  readonly finding: PipelineFinding;
   120	}
   121	
   122	function resolvedDuplicateFindings(doc: Document, lineCounter: LineCounter): readonly PositionedFinding[] {
   123	  const findings: PositionedFinding[] = [];
   124	  const visit = (node: unknown): void => {
   125	    if (isMap(node)) {
   126	      for (let right = 1; right < node.items.length; right += 1) {
   127	        const rightKey = node.items[right]?.key;
   128	        const earlierKeys = node.items.slice(0, right).map((pair) => pair.key);
   129	        const hasComposeTimeMatch = earlierKeys.some((leftKey) => yamlNodeEqual(leftKey, rightKey));
   130	        const hasResolvedMatch = !hasComposeTimeMatch
   131	          && earlierKeys.some((leftKey) => yamlNodeEqual(leftKey, rightKey, doc));
   132	        if (!hasResolvedMatch) continue;
   133	
   134	        const range = (rightKey as { readonly range?: readonly number[] } | null)?.range;
   135	        const offset = range?.[0] ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
   136	        const pos = Number.isSafeInteger(offset) ? lineCounter.linePos(offset) : undefined;
   137	        findings.push({
   138	          offset,
   139	          finding: {
   140	            stage: "parse",
   141	            ...(pos && pos.line > 0 ? { line: pos.line, col: pos.col } : {}),
   142	            message: "Map keys must be unique",
   143	          },
   144	        });
   145	      }
   146	      for (const pair of node.items) visit(pair);
   147	    } else if (isSeq(node)) {
   148	      for (const item of node.items) visit(item);
   149	    } else if (isPair(node)) {
   150	      visit(node.key);
   151	      visit(node.value);
   152	    }
   153	  };
   154	  visit(doc.contents);
   155	  return findings;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/** E1 (draft C20): positional parse findings — 1-based line/col where the parser provides them. */
   159	function parseFinding(error: YAMLError): PipelineFinding {
   160	  const pos = error.linePos?.[0];
   161	  return {
   162	    stage: "parse",
   163	    ...(pos ? { line: pos.line, col: pos.col } : {}),
   164	    message: error.message,
   165	  };
   166	}
   167	
   168	export function loadTemplate(bytes: Uint8Array, opts: LoadTemplateOptions = {}): TemplateLoadResult {
   169	  // READ stage, decode half (G3/draft C6): strict UTF-8 — invalid bytes
   170	  // reject with a content-free message, never the silent U+FFFD repair.
   171	  let text: string;
   172	  try {
   173	    text = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
   174	  } catch {
   175	    return fail("read", [
   176	      {
   177	        stage: "read",
   178	        ...(opts.path !== undefined ? { path: opts.path } : {}),
   179	        message: "invalid UTF-8 byte sequence (templates are strict UTF-8)",
   180	      },
   181	    ]);
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  // PARSE stage (G4–G7/draft C2–C4, C34): the document API with BOTH
   185	  // errors AND warnings promoted (fail-closed), plus the directive
   186	  // check — its synthesized finding HEADS the ordered list.
   187	  let doc: ReturnType<typeof parseDocument>;
   188	  const lineCounter = new LineCounter();
   189	  try {
   190	    doc = parseDocument(text, {
   191	      lineCounter,
   192	      uniqueKeys: (a: ParsedNode, b: ParsedNode) => yamlNodeEqual(a, b),
   193	    });
   194	  } catch (error) {
   195	    // Defensive: probes P9/P13 show content diagnostics collect in
   196	    // doc.errors — the stage catch is the C22 every-stage belt.
   197	    return fail("parse", [{ stage: "parse", message: errorMessage(error) }]);
   198	  }
   199	  const parseFindings: PipelineFinding[] = [];
   200	  const yamlDirective = doc.directives?.yaml;
   201	  if (yamlDirective?.explicit === true && yamlDirective.version !== "1.2") {
   202	    // The silently-ADOPTED case (probe P17: %YAML 1.1 — zero errors,
   203	    // zero warnings, the full 1.1 trap restored). Synthesized: the
   204	    // parser emitted nothing, so no position fields exist.
   205	    parseFindings.push({
   206	      stage: "parse",
   207	      message: `%YAML ${yamlDirective.version} directive: only YAML 1.2 is supported`,
   208	    });
   209	  }
   210	  const positionedErrors: PositionedFinding[] = [
   211	    ...doc.errors.map((error) => ({ offset: error.pos[0], finding: parseFinding(error) })),
   212	    ...resolvedDuplicateFindings(doc, lineCounter),
   213	  ].sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset);
   214	  for (const { finding } of positionedErrors) {
   215	    parseFindings.push(finding);
   216	  }
   217	  for (const warning of doc.warnings) {
   218	    parseFindings.push(parseFinding(warning));
   219	  }
   220	  if (parseFindings.length > 0) {
   221	    return fail("parse", parseFindings);
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  // RESOLVE stage (G8/draft C5+C36): toJS — the alias-amplification
   225	  // guard throws HERE, invisible to doc.errors (probes P8/P14/P16).
   226	  let value: unknown;
   227	  try {
   228	    // Preserve resolved map-key types through validation. The default
   229	    // object materialization would collapse distinct YAML keys such as
   230	    // `1` and `"1"` before V5 can reject the non-string key.
   231	    value = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
   232	  } catch (error) {
   233	    return fail("resolve", [{ stage: "resolve", message: errorMessage(error) }]);
   234	  }
   235	
   236	  // VALIDATE stage: ONE run of the template surface's declared schema
   237	  // (ADR-019 D1) over the resolved value graph, on the FILE channel so
   238	  // the source-bearing lanes have an operand. The two rungs this stage
   239	  // used to have — the source-form walk and the admission rung — are one
   240	  // computation over one declaration; their findings were already
   241	  // required to accumulate in ONE `validate` result (ch11-P4 F7), and
   242	  // now they cannot fail to. The result stays XOR (ch8-C22): an admitted
   243	  // template escapes only when the run is findings-free.
   244	  try {
   245	    const admitted = admitFromSource(value, doc, text, opts.catalog ?? EMPTY_CATALOG);
   246	    if (!admitted.ok) {
   247	      return fail("validate", admitted.findings);
   248	    }
   249	    return { ok: true, template: admitted.template };
   250	  } catch (error) {
   251	    // The C22 every-stage belt: no known input reaches this (V15's
   252	    // cycle rule is a FINDING, not a throw), but the stage still maps.
   253	    return fail("validate", [{ path: "$", message: `internal validator failure: ${errorMessage(error)}` }]);
   254	  }
   255	}

### F5 — A deklarált substrate-szabályok nagy része díszadat; a `load.ts` külön, hard-coded authority maradt

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — Ez egyszerre deklarált attribútumok figyelmen kívül hagyása és deklarációtól független kézi viselkedés a megváltozott fájlokban.

WHAT: A `templateFormat.substrate` blokkból a runtime csak a ciklusüzenetet olvassa; a strict UTF-8, YAML-verzió, duplicate-key üzenet, finding order és internal-failure viselkedés deklarációs értékei helyett a `load.ts` saját literálokat és ágakat használ.

WHERE: `v3/src/definition/load.ts:168`

EVIDENCE:
```text
$ rg -n 'templateFormat\.substrate|substrate\.' v3/src/definition/load.ts v3/src/definition/schema/*.ts

v3/src/definition/schema/engine.test.ts:38:    substrate: templateFormat.substrate,
```

WHY IT MATTERS: A declaration módosítása ezeknél a strukturális betöltési szabályoknál nem módosítja a tényleges viselkedést, miközben az olvasó a teljes `SubstrateDecl` blokkot kanonikus, fogyasztott authoritynek látja; ez pontosan false-green deklaráció.

FIX SKETCH: Új machinery nélkül a legkisebb korrekció az, hogy a `load.ts` meglévő ágai a már deklarált `templateFormat.substrate` értékeket olvassák a duplikált literálok és rögzített sorrend helyett; a YAML-könyvtár által birtokolt szövegeket továbbra sem kell deklarációból generálni.

   296	    mutant: fixed("root", { v: { kind: "integer", tag: "v", rows: ROWS } }),
   297	    yaml: "v: 0x10\n",
   298	    expected: { path: "v", message: 'v must be written as a plain decimal integer >= 1; got source form "0x10"' },
   299	  },
   300	  {

   302	    decl: { kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: ROWS, members: [{ value: "a" }, { value: "b" }], message: "{path} must be one of {members}; got {value}" },
   303	    mutant: { kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: ROWS, members: [{ value: "a" }, { value: "b" }, { value: "c" }], message: "{path} must be one of {members}; got {value}" },

   305	    expected: { path: "$", message: '$ must be one of a, b; got "c"' },
   306	  },

   336	      valueClasses: {

   347	    expected: { path: "$", message: "$ must be a map when present; got 3" },
   348	  },
   349	  {
   350	    claim: "channel — a DIRECT-only key is an unknown key on the file channel",
   351	    decl: fixed("root", { a: text("a"), produced: { kind: "raw", tag: "produced", rows: ROWS, channel: "direct" } }),
   352	    mutant: fixed("root", { a: text("a"), produced: { kind: "raw", tag: "produced", rows: ROWS } }),

   354	    expected: { path: "produced", message: 'unknown key "produced"' },
   355	  },
   356	  {
   357	    claim: "channel — a FILE-only enum member is refused on the direct channel",
   358	    decl: { kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: ROWS, members: [{ value: "authored", channel: "file" }, { value: "stored", channel: "direct" }], message: "{path} must be one of {members}; got {value}" },
   359	    mutant: { kind: "enum", tag: "e", rows: ROWS, members: [{ value: "authored" }, { value: "stored" }], message: "{path} must be one of {members}; got {value}" },

   361	    expected: { path: "$", message: '$ must be one of stored; got "authored"' },
   362	  },
   363	];
   364	
   365	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   366	
   367	function run(guard: Guard, decl: NodeDecl): readonly ValidationFinding[] {
   368	  return guard.yaml === undefined
   369	    ? direct(decl, guard.value, guard.extra, guard.catalog)
   370	    : fromFile(decl, guard.yaml, guard.extra);
   371	}
   372	
   373	describe("the engine's vocabulary guards, each with a discriminating fixture", () => {
   374	  for (const guard of GUARDS) {
   375	    it(`${guard.claim} — fires`, () => {
   376	      expect(run(guard, guard.decl)).toContainEqual(guard.expected);
   377	    });
   378	
   379	    it(`${guard.claim} — the fixture DISCRIMINATES (the mutant declaration does not fire it)`, () => {
   380	      expect(run(guard, guard.mutant)).not.toContainEqual(guard.expected);
   381	    });
   382	  }
   383	
   384	  it("the guard register is complete, unique and PINNED", () => {
   385	    // One level: the fixture list above IS the register. The pin makes
   386	    // adding or dropping a guard a visible edit rather than a drift.
   387	    expect(GUARDS).toHaveLength(31);
   388	    expect(new Set(GUARDS.map((guard) => guard.claim)).size).toBe(GUARDS.length);
   389	    for (const guard of GUARDS) {
   390	      expect(guard.decl).not.toStrictEqual(guard.mutant);
   391	    }
   392	  });
   393	});
   394	
   395	describe("lane order is DECLARED, because the measured nodes disagree", () => {
   396	  const node = (order: "missingThenUnknown" | "unknownThenPerKey" | "unknownThenMissingThenValues"): NodeDecl =>
   397	    fixed(
   398	      "m",
   399	      {
   400	        a: text("a", { presence: { required: true }, grammar: { re: "^ok$", message: "a bad" } }),
   401	        b: text("b", { presence: { required: true } }),
   402	      },
   403	      { laneOrder: order, missingMessage: 'missing "{key}"' },
   404	    );
   405	  const value = { a: "no", surplus: 1 };
   406	
   407	  it("missingThenUnknown — every missing key, then every unknown key, then the value lanes", () => {
   408	    expect(direct(node("missingThenUnknown"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
   409	      'missing "b"',
   410	      'unknown key "surplus"',
   411	      "a bad",
   412	    ]);
   413	  });
   414	
   415	  it("unknownThenPerKey — unknown keys, then each key's missing-or-value lane in declared order", () => {
   416	    expect(direct(node("unknownThenPerKey"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
   417	      'unknown key "surplus"',
   418	      "a bad",
   419	      'missing "b"',
   420	    ]);
   421	  });
   422	
   423	  it("unknownThenMissingThenValues — unknown keys, then every missing key, then the value lanes", () => {
   424	    expect(direct(node("unknownThenMissingThenValues"), value).map((f) => f.message)).toStrictEqual([
   425	      'unknown key "surplus"',
   426	      'missing "b"',
   427	      "a bad",
   428	    ]);
   429	  });
   430	});
   431	
   432	describe("suppression: the implicit container precondition and declared gating", () => {
   433	  const root = fixed("root", {
   434	    steps: {
   435	      kind: "map.open",
   436	      tag: "steps",
   437	      rows: ROWS,
   438	      containerMessage: "steps must be a map",
   439	      keyLaneAt: "container",
   440	      entry: text("step"),
   441	    },
   442	    start: {
   443	      kind: "string",
   444	      tag: "start",
   445	      rows: ROWS,
   446	      memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.steps" }, message: "start must name a step" },
   447	    },
   448	  });
   449	
   450	  it("a WRONG-KIND container yields its own finding and suppresses the rule that selects over it", () => {
   451	    const findings = direct(root, { steps: 7, start: "nope" });
   452	    expect(findings).toStrictEqual([{ path: "steps", message: "steps must be a map" }]);
   453	  });
   454	
   455	  it("a MISSING container suppresses the same rule", () => {
   456	    expect(direct(root, { start: "nope" })).toStrictEqual([]);
   457	  });
   458	
   459	  it("an EMPTY container does NOT suppress it — the set exists and is empty", () => {
   460	    expect(direct(root, { steps: {}, start: "nope" })).toStrictEqual([
   461	      { path: "start", message: "start must name a step" },
   462	    ]);
   463	  });
   464	
   465	  it("a declared `gating` key class makes the selector's operand unreliable", () => {
   466	    const gated = fixed("root", {
   467	      steps: {
   468	        kind: "map.open",
   469	        tag: "steps",
   470	        rows: ROWS,
   471	        containerMessage: "steps must be a map",
   472	        keyClass: { ...(idish as { kind: "string" }), gating: true } as NodeDecl,
   473	        keyLaneAt: "container",
   474	        entry: text("step"),
   475	      } as NodeDecl,
   476	      start: {
   477	        kind: "string",
   478	        tag: "start",
   479	        rows: ROWS,
   480	        memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.steps" }, message: "start must name a step" },
   481	      },
   482	    });
   483	    const messages = direct(gated, { steps: { BAD: "x" }, start: "nope" }).map((f) => f.message);
   484	    expect(messages).toContain("steps: bad id");
   485	    expect(messages).not.toContain("start must name a step");
   486	  });
   487	});
   488	
   489	describe("selectors", () => {
   490	  const root = fixed("root", {
   491	    a: { kind: "map.open", tag: "a", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("ae") },
   492	    b: { kind: "list", tag: "b", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", memberLaneAt: "index", member: text("be") },
   493	    pick: {
   494	      kind: "string",
   495	      tag: "pick",
   496	      rows: ROWS,
   497	      memberOf: {
   498	        relation: "memberOf",
   499	        target: { union: [{ keysOf: "$.a" }, { valuesOf: "$.b" }] },
   500	        message: "pick must be in a or b",
   501	      },
   502	    },
   503	  });
   504	
   505	  it("a union of keys(..) and values(..) accepts a member of EITHER", () => {
   506	    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "y" })).toStrictEqual([]);
   507	    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "x" })).toStrictEqual([]);
   508	  });
   509	
   510	  it("a member of NEITHER is a finding", () => {
   511	    expect(direct(root, { a: { x: "1" }, b: ["y"], pick: "z" })).toStrictEqual([
   512	      { path: "pick", message: "pick must be in a or b" },
   513	    ]);
   514	  });
   515	
   516	  it("an operand not yet evaluated is DEFERRED, not read early", () => {
   517	    // `pick` is declared BEFORE `a`, so its operand is pending when the
   518	    // field is reached; a naive engine reads an empty set and reports.
   519	    const early = fixed("root", {
   520	      pick: {
   521	        kind: "string",
   522	        tag: "pick",
   523	        rows: ROWS,
   524	        memberOf: { relation: "memberOf", target: { keysOf: "$.a" }, message: "pick must be in a" },
   525	      },
   526	      a: { kind: "map.open", tag: "a", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("ae") },
   527	    });
   528	    expect(direct(early, { pick: "x", a: { x: "1" } })).toStrictEqual([]);
   529	    expect(direct(early, { pick: "zz", a: { x: "1" } })).toStrictEqual([
   530	      { path: "pick", message: "pick must be in a" },
   531	    ]);
   532	  });
   533	
   534	  it("the `^` root resolves from the citing node's OWN container, never from itself", () => {
   535	    const nested = fixed("root", {
   536	      step: fixed("step", {
   537	        edges: { kind: "map.open", tag: "edges", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("t") },
   538	        gates: {
   539	          kind: "map.open",
   540	          tag: "gates",
   541	          rows: ROWS,
   542	          containerMessage: "c",
   543	          keyLaneAt: "container",
   544	          keysSubsetOf: { relation: "keysSubsetOf", target: { keysOf: "^.edges" }, message: "dead config: '{key}'" },
   545	          entry: text("g"),
   546	        },
   547	      }),
   548	    });
   549	    expect(direct(nested, { step: { edges: { GO: "x" }, gates: { GO: "g" } } })).toStrictEqual([]);
   550	    expect(direct(nested, { step: { edges: { GO: "x" }, gates: { NOPE: "g" } } })).toStrictEqual([
   551	      { path: "step.gates.NOPE", message: "dead config: 'NOPE'" },
   552	    ]);
   553	  });
   554	});
   555	
   556	describe("the equals cross rule reports each direction at its own grain", () => {
   557	  const root = fixed("root", {
   558	    declared: { kind: "map.open", tag: "declared", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("d") },
   559	    users: { kind: "map.open", tag: "users", rows: ROWS, containerMessage: "c", keyLaneAt: "container", entry: text("u") },
   560	  });
   561	  const extra: Partial<SurfaceDecl> = {
   562	    crossRules: [
   563	      {
   564	        tag: "roleset",
   565	        rows: ROWS,
   566	        relation: "equals",
   567	        left: { keysOf: "$.declared" },
   568	        right: { collect: "$.users.*" },
   569	        missingFromLeft: { at: "declared", message: "{valueJson} is used but not declared" },
   570	        missingFromRight: { at: "declared.{valueRaw}", message: "{valueJson} is declared but not used" },
   571	      },
   572	    ],
   573	  };
   574	
   575	  it("used-but-undeclared reports at the container", () => {
   576	    expect(direct(root, { declared: {}, users: { u1: "ghost" } }, extra)).toStrictEqual([
   577	      { path: "declared", message: '"ghost" is used but not declared' },
   578	    ]);
   579	  });
   580	
   581	  it("declared-but-unused reports at the ENTRY", () => {
   582	    expect(direct(root, { declared: { spare: "x" }, users: {} }, extra)).toStrictEqual([
   583	      { path: "declared.spare", message: '"spare" is declared but not used' },
   584	    ]);
   585	  });
   586	
   587	  it("a matched set produces nothing, and duplicates on the used side do not double-report", () => {
   588	    expect(direct(root, { declared: { r: "x" }, users: { a: "r", b: "r" } }, extra)).toStrictEqual([]);
   589	  });
   590	});
   591	
   592	describe("the delegate hand-off", () => {
   593	  const binding = fixed("binding", {
   594	    uses: text("uses", { presence: { required: true, foldedIntoTypeLane: true }, typeMessage: "uses must be a string" }),
   595	    config: {
   596	      kind: "delegate",
   597	      tag: "config",
   598	      rows: ROWS,
   599	      registry: "gateCatalog",
   600	      by: "uses",
   601	      presence: { required: true, foldedIntoTypeLane: true },
   602	      dependsOn: ["uses"],
   603	      beltMessage: "evaluator '{valueRaw}' reported a config failure without findings",
   604	    },
   605	  });
   606	  const registration = (result: unknown): GateRegistration =>
   607	    ({
   608	      implementation: "declarative",
   609	      execution: "inline",
   610	      requiresRuntimeContext: false,
   611	      validateAndNormalizeConfig: () => result,
   612	      evaluate: () => ({ verdict: "allow" }),
   613	    }) as unknown as GateRegistration;
   614	  const catalogOf = (result: unknown): GateCatalog => ({ resolve: () => registration(result) });
   615	
   616	  it("the registration's config-relative findings are PREFIXED with the binding's address", () => {
   617	    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "value", message: "value is required", code: "c1" }] });
   618	    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b", config: {} }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
   619	      { path: "config.value", message: "value is required", code: "c1" },
   620	    ]);
   621	  });
   622	
   623	  it("a config-relative path of \"\" addresses the config object itself", () => {
   624	    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] });
   625	    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b", config: 3 }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
   626	      { path: "config", message: "config must be a map" },
   627	    ]);
   628	  });
   629	
   630	  it("the delegation BELT: a failure reported with ZERO findings still blocks", () => {
   631	    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [] });
   632	    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b", config: {} }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
   633	      { path: "config", message: "evaluator 'a.b' reported a config failure without findings" },
   634	    ]);
   635	  });
   636	
   637	  it("a failed `uses` SUPPRESSES the hand-off — nothing is resolved against a broken id", () => {
   638	    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] });
   639	    expect(direct(binding, { uses: 9, config: {} }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
   640	      { path: "uses", message: "uses must be a string" },
   641	    ]);
   642	  });
   643	
   644	  it("an ABSENT config still reaches the registration — presence is the evaluator's business", () => {
   645	    const catalog = catalogOf({ ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "a config is required" }] });
   646	    expect(direct(binding, { uses: "a.b" }, undefined, catalog)).toStrictEqual([
   647	      { path: "config", message: "a config is required" },
   648	    ]);
   649	  });
   650	});
   651	
   652	describe("the normalizer (ADR-019 D3) — derivation, never validation", () => {
   653	  it("expands a COMPLETE per-edge flag map, all-false when no advancing set is declared", () => {
   654	    const value = {
   655	      steps: {
   656	        a: { transitions: { GO: "b" } },
   657	        b: { transitions: {} },
   658	      },
   659	    };
   660	    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
   661	    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: false });
   662	    expect((value.steps.b as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({});
   663	  });
   664	
   665	  it("expands against the DECLARED advancing set", () => {
   666	    const value = {
   667	      round: { advanceOnArrivalAt: ["b"] },
   668	      steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b", STAY: "a" } } },
   669	    };
   670	    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
   671	    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: true, STAY: false });
   672	  });
   673	
   674	  it("PRODUCER MONOPOLY: a pre-populated flag map is recomputed, never trusted", () => {
   675	    const value = { steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b" }, advancesRound: { GO: true, PHANTOM: true } } } };
   676	    normalize(templateFormat, value, new Map());
   677	    expect((value.steps.a as Record<string, unknown>)["advancesRound"]).toStrictEqual({ GO: false });
   678	  });
   679	
   680	  it("writes each registration's EFFECTIVE config into the binding's single config surface", () => {
   681	    const value = {
   682	      steps: { a: { transitions: { GO: "b" }, gates: { GO: [{ uses: "x.y", config: { authored: 1 } }] } } },
   683	    };
   684	    const effective = new Map<string, unknown>([["steps.a.gates.GO[0]", { resolved: 2 }]]);
   685	    normalize(templateFormat, value, effective);
   686	    expect((value.steps.a.gates as Record<string, unknown>)["GO"]).toStrictEqual([
   687	      { uses: "x.y", config: { resolved: 2 } },
   688	    ]);
   689	  });
   690	});
   691	
   692	describe("the declaration's own hygiene", () => {
   693	  it("every declaration tag is unique — the tag-closure check's mechanical half (D4)", () => {
   694	    const tags = collectTags(templateFormat);
   695	    expect(new Set(tags).size).toBe(tags.length);
   696	  });
   697	
   698	  it("every declared node cites at least one ratified row", () => {
   699	    const walk = (decl: NodeDecl): string[] => {
   700	      const own = decl.rows.length === 0 ? [decl.tag] : [];
   701	      switch (decl.kind) {
   702	        case "map.fixed":
   703	          return [...own, ...Object.values(decl.fields).flatMap(walk)];
   704	        case "map.open":
   705	          return [...own, ...(decl.keyClass ? walk(decl.keyClass) : []), ...walk(decl.entry)];
   706	        case "list":
   707	          return [...own, ...walk(decl.member)];
   708	        case "union":
   709	          return [...own, ...(decl.mapCase ? walk(decl.mapCase) : [])];
   710	        default:
   711	          return own;
   712	      }
   713	    };
   714	    expect(walk(templateFormat.root)).toStrictEqual([]);
   715	  });
   716	});

### F6 — Öt további vocabulary-mezőnek nincs runtime vagy hygiene-fogyasztója

CLASS: IN-SCOPE — A Lens F minden használó nélküli vocabulary-attribútum jelentését kéri.

WHAT: A `presence.required`, `unique.grain`, `resolvedForm.safeInteger`, `delegate.registry` és `SurfaceDecl.surface` mezőket a deklaráció és a tesztfixture-ek kitöltik, de a motor/normalizer egyiket sem olvassa; a viselkedést rendre a szülő objektum puszta jelenléte vagy a node `kind` ága implicit módon dönti el.
